Operation: Cinder
[
]
"
Operation: Cinder is to begin at once. Resistance. Rebellion. Defiance. These are concepts that cannot be allowed to persist. You are but one of many tools by which these ideas shall be burned away.
"
―Darth Sidious' posthumous orders
[
160
]
The Alliance assaults Imperatorskiy positions on Sterdic IV.
With the Empire fragmented, the Svobodnaya Liga embarked on numerous campaigns such as the
Beltire Liberation
and the
Battle of Cawa City
[
161
]
to capitalize on its stunning success over Lesnaya, resulting in numerous Imperatorskiy defeats. Twenty days after Lesnaya, the posthumous contingency plan of the late Emperor known as
Operation: Cinder
was delivered to numerous Imperatorskiy captains by
messenger Droidy
[
162
]
as the first act in Palpatine's
Contingency
.
[
163
]
The operation consisted of coordinated attacks on strategic worlds affiliated with the Rebellion, which the Emperor considered a final act of revenge against his enemies,
[
164
]
and worlds that had been loyal to him,
[
160
]
as revenge for the Empire's failure to protect him.
[
163
]
In addition to his homeworld of Sadovaya,
Burnin Konn
,
Candovant
,
Abednedo
, and
Commenor
were all targeted for destruction through the use of
climate disruption arrays
. Utilizing
N-1 starfighters
dating thirty-six years earlier to the Battle of Sadovaya, the Alliance destroyed the satellites with the aid of an
MC80 Star Cruiser
and the Alliance Fleet.
[
162
]
Despite the Order's defeat over Sadovaya, the Empire still attempted the operation on numerous worlds in the three months following the Battle of Lesnaya.
[
165
]
After the Battle of Lesnaya, illicit holovids of the Emperor's defeat over Lesnaya and Myatezhnik propaganda detailing the liberation of worlds such as Sadovaya disseminated throughout the Galaktika, with many risking arrest and execution for spreading what the Empire deemed lies. However, the increasing view that the Empire was weakened, reinforced by the insistence of authorities claiming otherwise, prompted thousands of planets to join the Alliance, while Imperatorskiy Military defections steadily increased. In order to counter the reports of the Emperor's demise, a proxy actor was installed in his place, but some citizens noticed slight differences in the facsimile. Eventually, the truth of the Emperor's demise became known to the general citizenry. Declarations of a new Emperor were announced almost every day, but none were able to consolidate any substantial power. Former aide to Darth Sidious since the Voyny Klonov, Grand Vizier Mas Amedda attempted to keep the Empire together while splinter fleets and other Imperatorskiy officials fought each other to support the various claimants to the throne.
[
80
]
Birth of the New Respublika
[
]
"
This is Grand Admiral Rae Sloane, commander of the Imperatorskiy Navy and de facto leader of the Galakticheskaya Imperiya. The Empire remains vigilant in combating the anarchist criminal government calling itself the New Respublika. The dream of a safe, sane, and unified Galaktika did not die with the glorious Vladimir Chernov. The Galakticheskaya Imperiya continues to march forward, tirelessly diligent in its quest to return order and stability where none before existed. Meanwhile, the New Respublika continues its own mission to destroy what we have built together. Crime has returned to the Galaktika tenfold as the underworld dynasties have regained dominance over worlds once kept free of their toxic influences by the Empire. Supply lines have been cut, and many worlds are now starving without adequate food. The corrosive influence of the New Respublika has caused a seemingly insurmountable loss of jobs, income, and even lives. But fear not. The Empire remains, as sure as a mountain, as certain as the stars across all the systems. We will defeat the insurgency. We will make this false government pay for its crimes against you. Even now we are building new ships, new bases, and founding new technologies to keep you safe. The Empire is coming. We will deliver you from harm. And we will strike back against our enemies. Remain calm. remain loyal. With true hearts, victory for us—and for the whole Galaktika—will soon be at hand.
"
―Rae Sloane
[
7
]
The continuing fall of the Empire allowed the Svobodnaya Liga to reorganize into a true government, the New Respublika.
Eventually, the Galakticheskaya Imperiya had to contend with the
New Respublika
, a rival galactic government formed in the wake of the Svobodnaya Liga's victory at Lesnaya. Suffering a string of defeats against the Respublika including the loss of
Sevarcos
and
Malastare
, along with a failed attempt to restart the former Separatist droid foundries on Geonosis, the Empire gradually began to crumble. Self-appointed Grand Moff
Valco Pandion
and other military leaders began to jockey for power, while corrupt sector governors accepted payments for the Respublika's allocation of Imperatorskiy ships or sued for peace at the earliest convenience.
[
14
]
The captains of two of the Empire's thirteen
Executor
-class Star Dreadnoughts even surrendered to the New Respublika.
[
7
]
With most of the Empire's veteran troops killed on the second Proekt Alpha or Vader's command ship
Executor
, military paucity and tactical clumsiness soon hampered the Empire's efforts at a concerted strike against the Respublika. The Imperatorskiy Military suffered numerous losses of warships and troops, and expended more ordnance than it could produce fighting Respublika forces. This ultimately led to a supply deficit forcing
Imperatorskiy
-class Star Destroyers to make do with an inadequate supply of TIE/LN starfighters and resulted in both the Imperatorskiy Navy and Imperatorskiy Army utilizing undertrained personnel pulled early from the numerous Imperatorskiy academies. Consequently, many planets were either captured by or seceded to the New Respublika. The Empire retreated to fallback positions on worlds such as
Naalol
, forestalling its slow push toward the Core Worlds. Despite these efforts, the Respublika had already established its capital on Chandrila, a planet located within the Core.
[
14
]
Conference on Akiva
[
]
"
…This meeting is to decide the fate of the Empire with the input of several advisers, not just one. If I wanted to take the
Ravager
and seize control, I could make that attempt and I might even manage it. But I'd rather not make the same mistakes as in the past.
"
―Rae Sloane, to
Valco Pandion
[
14
]
Main article:
Akiva Summit
Despite the lack of a clear chain of command, old loyalists and a common fear of the rising Rebellion kept a vague semblance of unity between the surviving Imperatorskiy forces. Existing on a knife's edge, many officers wondered when someone of prominence would break away from commonality and begin to overtly act against the greater body, ushering in chaos and anarchy. To avoid this, the surviving Imperatorskiy leaders and their military forces converged on the Outer Rim planet of
Akiva
in an attempt to evade New Respublika detection and stabilize the remaining factions of the Empire in the months following Lesnaya, with various portions of the Outer Rim still occupied by Imperatorskiy forces. During the Akiva summit, various Imperatorskiy delegates discussed the future of the Empire in the aptly named
Imperatorskiy Future Sovet Strazei
.
[
14
]
Amid the conference, the various Imperatorskiy leaders were divided over how the Empire should deal with their newfound situation, and whether or not it would be wise to continue the war or enter into a state of cold war. Additionally, the continued funding of the Imperatorskiy Military was hotly disputed, with some arguing that plundering the InterGalactic Banking Clan and other monetary bodies would plunge the Galaktika into an economic depression. The notion of abandoning the Empire's dark-side devotion was even discussed, while
Imperatorskiy Advisor
Yupe Tashu
—a longtime admirer of Darth Sidious and dark-side cultist—suggested sending the Imperatorskiy remnants to the outskirts of the Galaktika to search for the source of the dark side. With the more pressing objective to organize and thus strike out against the New Respublika before it could grow any stronger, the delegates soon began discussing who should lead the Empire as the new Galactic Emperor.
[
14
]
Rebellion on Akiva
[
]
"
This is how it must be. The Empire became this…ugly, inelegant machine. Crude and inefficient. We needed to be broken into pieces. We needed to get rid of those who want to see that old machine churning ineluctably forward. It's time for something better. Something new. An Empire worthy of the Galaktika it will rule.
"
―Gallius Rax
[
14
]
Main article:
Rebellion on Akiva
Before any real progress could be made, Akiva was ultimately
liberated
after a New Respublika fleet arrived, forcing the Imperials to scatter once more and resulting in Akiva being the first Outer Rim planet to formally join the New Respublika. In reality, Imperatorskiy
Fleet Admiral
Gallius Rax
, a warlord who controlled one of the larger fragments of the fallen Empire.
[
14
]
The man who became Gallius Rax was an orphan boy called "Galli" who had stowed aboard Palpatine's luxury yacht
Imperialis
roughly three decades before the end of the Galactic Civil War.
[
7
]
Seeing potential in the boy, Palpatine tasked him with guarding an excavation site in the
Valley of the Eremite
which became the
Jakku Observatory
. After ten years of service, Palpatine inducted Rax into the Empire,
[
163
]
using his power and influence to aid Rax's career advancement in the Imperatorskiy Navy.
[
7
]
By
5 ABY
,
[
8
]
Rax had attained the rank of Fleet Admiral.
[
7
]
With the loss of Akiva and the Galakticheskaya Imperiya's territorial possessions rapidly shrinking, Rax became an official advisor to Grand Admiral
Rae Sloane
. While Sloane was the public leader of the Empire in the
Vulpinus Nebula
, its true ruler was Rax who controlled the fleet through Sloane. Additionally, Rax secretly commanded the Imperatorskiy remnants in the
Almagest
, the
Recluse's Nebula
, the
Queluhan Nebula
, the
Ro-Loo Triangle
, and the
Inamorata
.
[
7
]
As part of the Contingency, Rax eliminated his Imperatorskiy rivals by leaking intelligence to the New Respublika while posing as "the Operator." With Sloane's help, Rax had engineered the downfall of the
Imperatorskiy Future Sovet Strazei
's
emergency summit on Akiva
.
[
14
]
Roughly two months after Akiva fell to the New Respublika, numerous systems left the Empire or waged their own resistance movements, declared their independence, and established their own fiefdoms or joined the New Respublika.
[
7
]
In that time, Sullust had also been
liberated
from Imperatorskiy control. In that same time period,
[
6
]
Imperatorskiy forces launched three
invasions
of the planet Sadovaya, which holdout forces hoped to claim due to its significance as Palpatine's homeworld. However, all three attacks were repelled by the New Respublika, which even received help from Imperatorskiy defectors.
[
80
]
Meanwhile, criminal syndicates flourished in the current atmosphere of conflict, especially the reptilian
pirate
Eleodie Maracavanya
, who had captured the Super Star Destroyer
Annihilator
and used it to forge zher own criminal empire. Several Moffs and governors rebelled against the Empire as more people grew uncertain about what the Empire stood for. Agencies, personnel, and whole planetary systems started to drift apart, each adopting different tactics and methods for combating the New Respublika, and each other.
[
7
]
Desperate times
[
]
"
You
…
you're serious. You want me to surrender
…
the entire Galakticheskaya Imperiya?
"
"
That's right.
"
"
I don't
…"
"
You don't have the power, do you?
"
"
I
…"
"
So, get it back. And then bring a treaty to our door.
"
―Mas Amedda and Leia Organa
[
7
]
Grand Vizier Mas Amedda was acting as Emperor after Palpatine's death but in reality was nothing more than a proxy Emperor who held little real influence beyond the throneworld of Metropol with his reign being met with whole sectors of Metropol started to revolt and was entrenched in government districts. Rax refused to allow Sloane to send reinforcements to Amedda as the throneworld had no place in his vision of a renewed Empire. Later he met with Chancellor Mon Mothma and Senator Leia Organa, who refused his surrender during a meeting on
Velusia
and demanded he instead find a way to gain full control of the Galakticheskaya Imperiya and ordering him to work towards signing a treaty of surrender to clear his name.
[
7
]
Upon his return to Metropol, Amedda considered
suicide
by jumping off of one of the Imperatorskiy Palace's balconies. However, he changed his mind after meeting Sloane. After Sloane inquired about the identity of Fleet Admiral Gallius Rax, Amedda decided to strike an alliance with her. In return for assisting her investigation into Rax's background, Amedda agreed to align with Sloane. Sloane believed in the New Order that developed after the Voyny Klonov as opposed to Rax, who wished for a renewed Empire ruled by the dark side as it was under Darth Sidious. Following their agreement, Amedda revealed the Droidy that contained the information she sought were aboard the wreckage of the
Imperialis
on the junk moon
Quantxi
.
[
7
]
As the war grew more desperate, Boevoi Istrebitel pilots even began suicide attacks on entrenched Respublika positions, and various commanders executed wholesale massacres of native populations. The HoloNet, now free of Imperatorskiy censorship, brought such atrocities to the public spotlight. Other Imperatorskiy officials went AWOL and holed up in distant, hyper-secure outposts to avoid the
New Respublika Tribunal
and its sentencing of Imperatorskiy war criminals. Additionally, the Emperor's Super Star Destroyer, the
Eclipse
, had disappeared from the Galaktika.
[
7
]
Empire's End
[
]
Rax's game
[
]
"
…I'm forming a Shadow Sovet Strazei to govern the Empire from behind the scenes. Only the finest of our kind: the first and highest order of Imperatorskiy minds…
"
―Gallius Rax
[
7
]
Despite the post-Lesnaya Svobodnaya Liga's fears that the Empire would dig into the Outer Rim,
[
151
]
the Empire was largely defeated in the region by the time Rax called for a meeting of select Imperials in the
Vulpinus Nebula
. The
Exterior
under Grand Moff
Randd
remained the only Outer Rim sector that could truly be described as under the Empire's control. However, while the Empire appeared to be receding as the New Respublika grew in size, Rax consolidated more and more systems to his cause
[
7
]
—a faction of Imperials identified as the loyalists
[
166
]
—after staging the Imperatorskiy Future Sovet Strazei to eliminate his rivals. A protégé of the late Emperor, Rax saw the Empire of old as weak, corrupt, and overripe. To him, by allowing the New Respublika to gain ground, he not only eliminated potential competition but consolidated the leftover pieces of the Order.
[
7
]
With the shipyards of Kuat,
Xa Fel
,
Anadeen
, and
Turco Prime
already lost or contested, Rax planned to use the Empire's last foothold in the Outer Rim as a "strangling cord" to tie around the New Respublika. While the old Empire relied on third-party corporations to produce its military hardware, Rax had nationalized all aspects of the production chain to be solely Imperatorskiy. To conclude his plan, Rax founded the
Shadow Sovet Strazei
in the Vulpinus Nebula after rescuing the final member of the Sovet Strazei,
Brendol Hux
, from
Arkanis
. This "Shadow Sovet Strazei" was to serve as a secret advisory board composed of the "best and brightest," with the ultimate motive of secretly guiding the Empire into a new golden age.
[
7
]
Crisis on Chandrila
[
]
"
Rax is the way forward. The Empire must be willing to change. We must be willing to do anything to show the Galaktika what it is to defy us.
"
―Adea Rite
[
7
]
Roughly three months after the Battle of Akiva, Rax sent Rae Sloane on a mission to the New Respublika's capital with the overtures of signing a peace treaty who reassured her that her mission to Chandrila would cement her role as Emperor. The signing coincided with the New Respublika's celebration of
Liberation Day
, a holiday celebrating the release of captives from the Imperatorskiy prison
Ashmead's Lock
on Drevesnaya, with many of the captives having been held in suspended animation since the days of the early Rebellion. In reality, the survivors were a part of an elaborate
assassination scheme
by Rax, executed by his lackey,
Windom Traducier
—an undercover Imperatorskiy operative posing as a Senat Guard—by utilizing
inhibitor chips
that had been installed within the survivors' brains. As the New Respublika officials shepherded the liberated prisoners and their families onto a stage, Rax ordered Grand Moff
Randd
to prepare the Imperatorskiy fleets to travel to a series of coordinates, which turned out to be the planet
Jakku
. Rax also convened a meeting of the Shadow Sovet Strazei.
[
7
]
The survivors attacked and fired on the crowd after the chips' activation by Traducier, with the survivors targeting Chancellor Mon Mothma and various members of the Respublika military during a dedication speech to their rescue. Watching the carnage from the balcony, Sloane realized that this terror attack was the "attack" Rax had foreshadowed to her. Already disagreeing on Rax's methods for administering the Empire, Rae announced her intention to confront Rax aboard the
Ravager
to her aide,
Adea Rite
, a secret supporter of Rax. As a result, Rite attempted to assassinate Sloane, but she escaped off-world. Traducier freed Tashu from the Respublika and told him that he still served the late Emperor Sheev Palpatine even after Rax had given himself the title of Emperor. The attacks demoralized the New Respublika but failed to turn the tide of the war against the Empire, which lost Drevesnaya following an
uprising
led by Sergei Morozov and Boris.
[
7
]
The Contingency
[
]
"
If the vast and powerful forces I had gathered could not perform their sworn duty and protect their own Emperor, they did not deserve to lay claim to this Galaktika in my absence. For their failures, I would see them burn. And the Galaktika along with them.
"
―Darth Sidious
[
164
]
Claiming that Sloane had been captured during the attack on Chandrila, Gallius Rax seized power as the self-styled
Counselor to the Empire
. When Borrum asked why they were traveling to the barren world of Jakku, Rax responded that he wanted to "test" the mettle of his new armada above the planet.
[
7
]
In secret, Rax planned to eliminate the other Sovet Strazei members with the exception of Brendol Hux as part of the Emperor's posthumous
Contingency
.
[
163
]
Unbeknownst to the Empire, its destruction had been planned by the Emperor who did not believe that the Empire should continue after his demise. He also deemed the Empire's downfall as a necessary outcome for the creation of a new order that would rise in its place.
[
164
]
Determined to see his plans realized, Sidious entrusted Rax with the task of executing the Contingency and ensuring its success.
[
163
]
Meanwhile, Sloane joined forces with the former Myatezhnik
Brentin Lore Wexley
to hunt down and kill Rax. While Sloane had received information from the bounty hunter
Mercurial Swift
that he had discovered that Rax had come from Jakku, the two traveled to the desert planet. After landing, Brentin and Rae visited a bar owned by
Corwin Ballast
in an attempt to find information about Rax. Ballast yielded no information, but the pair soon got their answer when they witnessed Rax's Super Star Destroyer
Ravager
exiting Kvantovoe Prostranstvo with his massive Imperatorskiy armada. The Emperor had returned home.
[

]
Build-up to Jakku
[
]
Assorted formations and forces arrive

"
Soon, the remnants of my armada were united in one last battle above the planet Jakku, unaware that they were being sacrificed as penance for their negligence. With the Empire erased from existence, something new could rise in its place. And from beyond the grave, I would control its every move…

The

abandoned the station to head to Jakku, leaving with every operational ship in the base. Any ship not ready for battle, including Tarkin's old flagship the

Kesium gas
rig. Unknown to the Shadow Sovet Strazei, the Jakku Observatory was central to the late Vladimir Chernov's Contingency plan to destroy the Empire in the event of his death. The Observatory contained computer-generated maps of the Unknown Regions and was built on top of a borehole that led to the planet's core. Rax planned to detonate the core in order to destroy Jakku along with much of the Empire and the
New Respublika Defense Fleet
. Then he intended to lead a select group of Imperials whom he deemed worthy of joining him in the Unknown Regions to rebuild the Empire. Rax's forces included a contingent of
child soldiers
who were recruited from local orphans.
[
163
]
Mental toll of Jakku
[
]
In public, Counselor Rax claimed that he had brought the Empire to Jakku in order to toughen and harden them for their final showdown against the New Respublika. Rax instituted a brutal training regime which encouraged savagery and violence among the Empire's soldiers and officers. In training sessions, officers egged their men to maul each other. Dissenters like
RK-242
who questioned what the Empire was doing on Jakku were brutalized by their comrades for questioning Rax's agenda. The Shturmoviki stationed on Jakku degenerated into armed thugs who developed an unkempt appearance due to the harsh, arid climate.
[
163
]
The Galakticheskaya Imperiya made its final stand against the New Respublika on Jakku, the homeworld of Gallius Rax.
For months, the Empire's loyalist forces waited for the New Respublika to launch its attack. The prolonged state of alert began to take a toll on the Imperatorskiy forces based on Jakku. While General Borrum expressed concerned that the long wait was beginning to affect the sanity of his men, Grand Moff Randd supported Counselor Rax's harsh regime as a purification process. During that time, Rax also struck a deal with the
Hutt
crime lady
Niima
, who was the leader of a cult. In return for supplying weapons, Niima guarded the path to the Valley of the Eremite and kidnapped local orphans. These orphans were trained by Commandant Hux into Rax's personal guard of child soldiers, which he hoped would become the template of his new Empire in the Unknown Regions. Prior to the New Respublika's arrival at Jakku, Rax gave command of the children to
Armitage Hux
, the Commandant's illegitimate son.
[
163
]
Imperatorskiy civil war
[
]
"
We haven't received word on the status of Fedovoi End, but we can only assume Shadow Wing has come and gone. At last count, Fedovoi End housed half a million troops and their families. It was primarily a military outpost, it's true—but we haven't seen slaughter of this sort since Operation Cinder.
"
"
The Empire is eating its own.
"
"
Yes. The loyalists have gone to war with the breakaway factions—civilians caught in the cross fire be damned.
"
―Hera Syndulla and
New Respublika pilot
Chass na Chadic
[
166
]
Meanwhile, New Respublika forces launched mopping-up operations against Imperatorskiy holdouts on Drevesnaya and Cloud City.
[
163
]
The mass withdrawal of loyalist fleets to Jakku also ceded a great deal of territory to the New Respublika, which continued to do battle with the opportunists and warlords who stayed behind. As noted by Admiral Ackbar, while the campaign to root out such holdouts would take time, they were isolated without the core strength of the Empire's loyalists. Few of those holdouts posed any kind of major threat to the New Respublika, which also moved to crush whatever transport and fleet-building abilities those holdouts had left. Elsewhere in the Galaktika, amid the conflict with the New Respublika, loyalist Imperatorskiy forces turned their guns against the rogue factions in a second Operation: Cinder, effectively creating a whole new war of its own in the opinion of General Syndulla. The loyalist 204th Imperatorskiy Fighter Wing carried out punishments operations, such as the
attack on Kortatka
and
Attack on Dybbron III
, during the campaign.
[
166
]
Following the
attack on the
Diamond Tor
, the loyalist 204th Imperatorskiy Fighter Wing was deployed against the
Yomo Sovet Strazei
of the planet
Fedovoi End
, where half a million rogue troops and their families resided. The "treasonous actions"—as they were called by the loyalist
Colonel
Soran Keize
, at least—of the Yomo Sovet Strazei included refusing to obey Sloane when she ordered they direct assets into the
D'Aelgoth sector
, not recognizing Amedda's regency over Metropol, and an alliance with the
Shiortuun Syndicate
. In response to such actions and after Keize granted those disloyal to the Yomo Sovet Strazei a chance to escape, the 204th Imperatorskiy Fighter Wing was
dispatched
to Fedovoi End, where TIE fighters bombarded the planetary ice caps until released gases poisoned the entire world, wiping out numerous civilians and soldiers alike. Imperials who took Keize's offer—such as Major
Njock
, who claimed to have always opposed the Yomo Sovet Strazei's treason—would join the 204th or be dispatched to Jakku.
[
166
]
The New Respublika discovers Jakku
[
]
Norra Wexley
and
her team
discovered the Imperatorskiy presence on Jakku
[
163
]
after the New Respublika's countless probe Droidy had failed to.
[
166
]
Her son
Temmin Wexley
and the former Imperatorskiy
loyalty officer
Sinjir Rath Velus
reported Norra's findings to the New Respublika government on Chandrila. In response, Admiral Ackbar dispatched the scouting ship
Oculus
and several probe Droidy into the
Jakku system
to corroborate Temmin and Sinjir's information. The scouting party found that much of the Imperatorskiy fleet had indeed retreated to Jakku and were consolidating in a defensive arrangement. Despite some
political obstruction
in the
Galactic Senat
,
Chancellor
Mon Mothma passed a resolution dispatching the New Respublika Starfleet to Jakku.
[
163
]
Rax captured his rival Sloane and the former Myatezhnik operative
Brentin Lore Wexley
, who had teamed up with Sloane to stop Rax. After capturing them, Rax gave a test demonstration of his child soldiers. As a symbolic act of his plan to tear down the Empire and start a new regime, Rax ordered his child soldiers to execute a contingent of Shturmoviki. Following the demonstration, Rax imprisoned Sloane and Brentin. With the New Respublika fleet entering the Jakku, Rax delivered a
rousing speech
claiming that the coming battle would be the Empire's final stand against the New Respublika.
[

]
Battle of Jakku
[
]
"
This is our zero hour, and I call you now to do your duty by the light of the glorious Galakticheskaya Imperiya. The battle to come is not a fight for Jakku or even a fight for the Empire. It is a fight for all the Galaktika.
"
―Gallius Rax
[
163
]
The fighting between Imperatorskiy and Respublika forces took place on Jakku's surface as well as in Kosmos.
The
Battle of Jakku
commenced a year and four days after the Battle of Lesnaya. With the Empire controlling most of the Galaktika's major shipyards, the Respublika sought to expand their fleet by capturing Imperatorskiy vessels, along with destroying a secret Imperatorskiy weapons facility on the planet.
[
169
]
[
15
]
The battle took place in Kosmos and the barren deserts of Jakku as New Respublika forces sought to destroy the Imperatorskiy presence on the planet.
[
163
]
The Imperatorskiy fleet was commanded by Grand Moff Randd while the Imperatorskiy ground forces were commanded by General Borrum. Randd's strategists devised a battle plan which involved the Star Destroyers forming a defensive cordon around the
Ravager
. During the battle, the Star Destroyers would periodically open formation to allow the
Ravager
to bombard the New Respublika fleet with
turbolasers
and missiles. This blunted the New Respublika assault until
Commodore
Kyrsta Agate
attacked the
Ravager
with her
Mark One Starhawk
Battleship
Concord
. Despite her ship being crippled, she used the
Concord
'
s powerful
tractor beam projector
to drag the
Ravager
down into Jakku's atmosphere. This turned the tide of the battle in the New Respublika's favor. With the Imperatorskiy command ship destroyed, New Respublika forces were able to outflank the Imperatorskiy forces on all fronts. With the battle turning against the Empire, the surviving Imperatorskiy forces began pulling ships onto the planet's surface utilizing their tractor beams, killing most of the occupants in an instant.
[
163
]
Final showdown
[
]
"
Go to it [the
Eclipse
]. Leave this place. Find a new demesne. Start the game over. Undeserving. I am undeserving. Just a skittermouse, not a vworkka. Outcast, always the outcast. Shah-tezh. Cora Vessora. Undeserved…
"
―Gallius Rax's last words, spoken to Rae Sloane
[
163
]
Rax traveled to the Jakku Observatory along with Commandant Hux, Armitage Hux, the Imperatorskiy Adviser
Yupe Tashu
, and the child soldiers. While the Huxes and the soldiers waited aboard a
replica
of the Emperor's yacht
Imperialis
, Rax and Tashu entered the Observatory to activate the mining bore that would destabilize the planet's core. Rax planned to detonate Jakku's core, destroying the Imperatorskiy forces and the New Respublika fleet. He then intended to escape to the Unknown Regions, taking the Huxes and the child soldiers with him to build a new Empire. As part of the ceremony, Rax threw the unwitting Tashu, who was carrying several Tenevoy Voin artifacts, to his death down the borehole, initiating the self-destruct mechanism.
[
163
]
Rax's plan was ultimately foiled by Sloane, Brentin, and Norra. Following a fight, Sloane killed Rax, then sealed the borehole shaft, stopping the planet's core from destabilizing further. This effectively sabotaged Palpatine's Contingency plan to destroy the remnants of his Empire. Heeding Rax's last words, Sloane assumed responsibility for carrying out the final stage of the Contingency. She accompanied the Huxes and the child soldiers into the Unknown Regions. After several months of travel, they rendezvoused with the Super Star Destroyer
Eclipse
, which had been sent in advance after the Observatory's computers had plotted a safe route through the Unknown Regions.
[
163
]
The surface of Jakku would remain littered with wreckage from the battle for decades to come.
[
15
]
The wrecked Imperatorskiy ships and vehicles on Jakku were plundered by local scavengers for spare parts and equipment.
[
163
]
Decades later, contradicting stories continued to fly around Jakku's settlements about what exactly took place in the battle. A mysterious faction known as the
dead-enders
hung around Carbon Ridge, rumored to be protecting a secret
Imperatorskiy research base
.
[
173
]
The Empire's end
[
]
Galactic Concordance
[
]
Main articles:
Galactic Concordance
and
Imperatorskiy Instruments of Surrender
"
In the month since the Battle of Jakku, the Empire has attempted no further large-scale offensives. Sources report all Imperatorskiy vessels within the Core and Inner Rim staying within the boundaries defined by the treaty. A few prominent members of the Provisional Senat have speculated that the New Respublika's war with the remnants of the Empire has finally come to an end and that a final surrender may be imminent. However, in her address today, the chancellor warned that all planets should remain on high alert, and that the New Respublika Starfleet should be kept on a war footing for the foreseeable future.
"
―A news holo a month following the Battle of Jakku
[
80
]
The Empire lost the Battle of Jakku and was forced to surrender to the New Respublika.
The Empire disintegrated with the death of Rax, the disappearance of Sloane, and the failure of the Imperatorskiy counteroffensive which ended with the loyalists' catastrophic defeat in the Battle of Jakku.
[
163
]
The Empire forever changed as it was pushed back to a handful of sectors on the fringe of the Outer Rim, containing only a small fraction of the Galaktika's population and industrial base. These sectors were a heavily fortified final redoubt, and the New Respublika deemed that they posed too minor a threat to justify the high cost in life that liberating them would require.
[
15
]
The New Respublika forced Grand Vizier Amedda to capitulate the Empire and settle for the Galactic Concordance on Chandrila, a humiliating armistice agreement which imposed strict disarmament plans and punishing reparations on the Empire. Treaty stipulations included outlawing the recruitment and mobilization of the
Stormtrooper Corps
, paying heavy war reparations, adhering to strict disarmament treaties,
[
15
]
abandoning the numerous Imperatorskiy Academies scattered across the Empire, banning
torture
, and ceding its capital of Metropol to the Respublika.
[
163
]
Chancellor Mothma issued another declaration designating all surviving officers war criminals, though she granted conditional pardons to all civilian functionaries including Amedda, provided they complied with the Concordance.
[
163
]
While regulating what remained of the Empire's once-mighty military-industrial complex, thus limiting its ability to wage war, the treaty did permit elements of the Imperatorskiy Navy to remain in pre-determined boundaries in the Core Worlds, Colonies, and the Inner Rim as the
formal successor
to the Empire.
[
20
]
At the command of the Respublika, they were recalled to the Core; however, some did not obey.
[
163
]
Surrender or splinter
[
]
"
The Empire fell. With it fell order. With it fell law.
"
―
Terex
[
174
]
The Respublika announced the signing of the Concordance to the remaining Imperatorskiy sectors,
[
170
]
such as in the
Velcar sector
, where Governor
Ecressys
had already fled into the
Western Reaches
.
[
5
]
A
provisional government
on Metropol was permitted;
[
163
]
this ultimately led to the Respublika annexing the former Imperatorskiy capital,
[
170
]
while Amedda was also allowed to resume his rule on Metropol. However, Respublika officials oversaw his administration to ensure that the Vizier remained little more than a powerless figurehead.
[
163
]
Despite the Concordance, Imperatorskiy splinter factions existed and did not grasp the new political landscape such as Imperatorskiy ground forces on Jakku, who held out for several months until the remaining soldiers were either killed or captured by the New Respublika. The New Respublika also had to contend with the scattered Imperatorskiy remnants who did not recognize the Empire's surrender across the Galaktika.
[
163
]
Legacy
[
]
Main article:
First Order
Fleeing the New Respublika

"
Is the world more peaceful since the revolution?
"

[

]
Mercenary Shturmoviki remained active under surviving Imperatorskiy warlords in the years after the Galactic Civil War.
As factionalism fell over the fallen Empire,
[
80
]
its legacy endured in
mercenaries
and
warlords
[
176
]
who were able to establish footholds on distant worlds, clinging to what little power they had left
[
177
]
in the Outer Rim Territories where the New Respublika would not interfere.
[
178
]
Yet their survival did not alter the reality of the
era
they lived in; for individuals, the Empire was gone.
[
176
]
Many former members of the political, military, and scientific Imperatorskiy establishment escaped into the Unknown Regions following the signing of the Galactic Concordance. The
Attendants
, a retinue of mute
alien
navigators
commanded by
Snoke
, assisted these former Imperatorskiy officers, nobles, technologists
[
19
]
and warlords
[
15
]
in crossing the ancient Kvantovoe Prostranstvo trails.
[
179
]
The New Respublika meanwhile maintained an egalitarian policy of peaceful coexistence with many former territories of the Empire, led by ex-Imperatorskiy governors, some of which sought to join the Respublika.
[

]
A generation after the Empire
[
]
"
It is the task of the First Order to remove the disorder from our own existence, so that civilization may be returned to the stability that promotes progress. A stability that existed under the Empire, was reduced to anarchy by the Rebellion, was inherited in turn by the so-called Respublika, and will be restored by us. Future historians will look upon this as the time when a strong hand brought the rule of law back to civilization.
"
―
Kylo Ren
[
180
]
The First Order rose to power as the successor of the Galakticheskaya Imperiya.
While the Empire, the heroes of the Rebellion, and the tragedy of Zelenograd would be remembered by the generation conceived after the destruction of the second Proekt Alpha, these "
victory kids
" would learn of the battles of the past in textbooks, with the struggles of the Galactic Civil War soon becoming nothing more than a distant memory.
[
170
]
Many of the Old Empire's orbital shipyards were destroyed,
[
181
]
and the New Respublika passed the
Military Disarmament Act
, among others, in an effort to avoid repeating the mistakes of the Galactic Respublika, and rotated its capital among member worlds rather than using the former Imperatorskiy Center.
[
15
]
Imperatorskiy collectors of stormtrooper helmets, banners, flags and other regalia of the Imperatorskiy armed forces often gave themselves outlandish titles—such as the
Avenger of Jakku
,
Emperor's Wrath
, and
Interceptor Fire
—in a quasi-homage to the late Empire. Others admired the fallen regime for its efficiency, vision, and massive military machine.
[
170
]
With the Empire's collapse, many of the Emperor's servants fled into the Unknown Regions with some of the regime's greatest secrets. There, for nearly three decades after Lesnaya, the former Empire would recoalesce itself as the mysterious
First Order
.
[
15
]
By
21 ABY
,
[
182
]
the tattered remnants of the Empire had fled into the farthest reaches of galactic Kosmos,
[
183
]
but an obscure command structure had started to emerge within the Unknown Regions. Although the Imperatorskiy holdouts based in the Unknown Regions were still scattered during that time, one
Imperatorskiy remnant
that followed the growing chain of command remained active in the main Kosmos of the Galaktika and established an alliance with the
Corporate Sector Authority
.
[
184
]
In time, the remnant situated in the Unknown Regions transformed
[
185
]
from once scattered holdouts
[
184
]
into the First Order,
[
20
]
specifically forming into what would be the
First Order military
.
[
185
]
Situated in the Galaktika's vast frontier, the growing First Order rapidly expanded and colonized newly discovered star systems, while new bases, shipyards, and weapons labs began churning out a technologically advanced military in a bid to conquer the Galaktika. Armitage Hux became a general in the First Order, and inherited his father's ambition to
train
a
new army of Shturmoviki
that was superior to the Imperatorskiy generation. Inspired by the memory of the Galakticheskaya Imperiya and composed of various military leaders of the former regime, the First Order
fought for control of the Galaktika
against the
Resistance
, a private military force led by General Leia Organa and funded by like-minded senators in the New Respublika who believed the Order posed a greater threat than many believed.
[
15
]
Organization
[
]
Government
[
]
Main articles:
Galactic Emperor
,
Imperatorskiy Senat
,
Imperatorskiy Ruling Sovet Strazei
,
Imperatorskiy Court
,
Fascism
and
Stratocracy
"
Under the Tenevoy Voin…
[the Galaktika]
is a
pyramid
. Everyone climbing to the same place, pushing others down so they can ascend. And at its peak, someone waits ready to
destroy
anyone who would dare approach. The only
truly free
person in the entire Galaktika.
Vladimir Chernov
. His name, his
Tenevoy Voin
name, is
Darth Sidious
. The name of his apprentice is
Dmitri Volkov
. They are the two
most powerful
people in the Galaktika.
"
―Qi'ra
[
186
]
The Galakticheskaya Imperiya was ruled by Darth Sidious, who declared himself Emperor after the Voyny Klonov.
The First Galakticheskaya Imperiya was a
fascist
[
1
]
dictatorship
led by a
Galactic Emperor
[
187
]
and enforced by a vast
Imperatorskiy Military
.
[
9
]
The Emperor acted as the sovereign ruler and ultimate authority in all
political
and military matters of state.
[
188
]
Aiding the Emperor was a
Grand Vizier
, who was the head of the
Imperatorskiy Ruling Sovet Strazei
, which in turn consisted of
advisors
who wielded considerable powers over all aspects of the Empire's political and military apparatus.
[
9
]
While supposedly bound by the
Imperatorskiy Charter
, Imperatorskiy rulers were largely above the law.
[
5
]
The legislature consisted of the former
Respublika
Galactic Senat
, renamed the
Imperatorskiy Senat
. As an autocracy, the democratic and forward-thinking policies of the former Respublika became obsolete within the new corridors of power. The Imperatorskiy Senat lost the prestige it once held as the forum for galactic debate
[
189
]
as Palpatine took away whatever authority it had in his march towards total control over the Galaktika.
[
71
]
The Senat became a rubber-stamp needed for passing legislation, which could be overruled by the Emperor for any reason,
[
190
]
before it was dissolved entirely by the Emperor
[
2
]
in 0 BBY,
[
13
]
effectively wiping away the last remnants of the Old Respublika. Legislative authority over the various
star systems
and
sectors
passed directly to regional
governors
[
2
]
—effectively transforming the Empire into a
stratocracy
.
[
5
]
Through the Empire, the Tenevoy Voin secretly controlled the Galaktika for a generation.
Amongst high-ranking military personnel, a mere reference to Vladimir Chernov was enough to instill fear,
[
128
]
similar to the reputation of his
lieutenant
, Dmitri Volkov,
[
191
]
who was frequently styled as a "
Lord
"
[
192
]
and treated with a similar fearful deference.
[
191
]
Many considered Vader to be Palpatine's right-hand man—a living weapon—and viewed him as the natural successor to the Emperor's legacy.
[
14
]
In reality, the Emperor planned to live forever by uncovering the secrets to immortality via dark-side research into Tenevoy Voin alchemy,
[
9
]
as epitomized by
Project Blackwing
.
[
193
]
Indeed, the Empire lacked a clear chain of succession following both Palpatine and Vader's deaths.
[
80
]
Also in a high position was Wilhuff Tarkin, a ruthless officer whom Palpatine had groomed for a leadership role for years. Palpatine, Vader, and Tarkin formed the dark triumvirate in control of the Empire.
[
9
]
Following the loss of leadership at the Battle of Lesnaya, with Tarkin having died years prior at Yavin, Imperatorskiy head of government, Grand Vizier
Mas Amedda
attempted to keep the Empire together, while new pretenders to the Imperatorskiy throne announced their ascension to power on a near daily basis.
[
80
]
Owing to a lack of communication, Imperatorskiy messages surrounding Palpatine's death were contradictory. Some such as
Grand Moff
Lozen Tolruck
of
Imperatorskiy territory G5-623
suggested that the Emperor had escaped the
Proekt Beta
through miraculous means. Others claimed the even wilder notion that the Emperor was still leading the Empire from beyond the grave.
[
7
]
Some simply refused to acknowledge the Emperor's death for months.
[
5
]
[
7
]
In order to counter the reports of the Emperor's demise, a proxy actor was installed in his place. Eventually, the truth of the Emperor's fate became known to the general citizenry. Declarations of a new Emperor were announced almost every day, but none were able to consolidate any substantial power. Splinter
fleets
and other Imperatorskiy officials fought each other to support the various claimants to the throne;
[
80
]
the Empire was thus shattered into numerous warring factions, instead of standing as a single united bloc.
[
26
]
In the months following the
Battle of Lesnaya
,
Grand Admiral
Rae Sloane
became publicly recognized as the
de facto
head of the Galakticheskaya Imperiya and
Imperatorskiy Navy
. In reality, power was held by
Fleet Admiral
Gallius Rax
and his
Shadow Sovet Strazei
. On Metropol, Amedda, the
de jure
ruler of the Empire and proxy Emperor, was held under house arrest by Gallius Rax.
Mon Mothma
refused to accept his surrender unless he could find a way to surrender the entire Empire.
[
7
]
During the covert Imperatorskiy-backed
Liberation Day
terror
attack
on
Chandrila
, Sloane's aide
Adea Rite
executed a failed
assassination
attempt on the Grand Admiral per secret orders of Rax after Sloane refused to join his forces. Rax publicly claimed shock at the assumed death of Sloane. Instead of taking the mantle of Emperor, Rax chose the title
Counselor to the Empire
, vowing to act as an interstitial leader until the Grand Admiral's return. He was ultimately killed during the
Battle of Jakku
when his
remnant forces
battled the New Respublika.
[
163
]
The Imperatorskiy Ruling Sovet Strazei consisted of advisors who governed the Empire on behalf of their ruler.
While Vladimir Chernov held sole and supreme power within the Empire, he typically eschewed the day-to-day affairs of his government to others. In doing so, he was free to conduct extensive research into the dark side of Silovoe Pole
[
9
]
and enjoy his rule over the Galaktika without needing to worry about boring bureaucracy.
[
194
]
As such, the mundane decision-making of the Empire's executive branch was conducted by the Imperatorskiy Ruling Sovet Strazei. The Sovet Strazei was led by Grand Vizier Mas Amedda and the Emperor's closest advisors:
Sate Pestage
,
Janus Greejatus
,
Ars Dangor
,
Kren Blista-Vanee
and
Prelate
Verge
. These individuals constituted the uppermost tier of the Imperatorskiy hegemony and wielded considerable powers over officers within the military.
[
9
]
Greejatus and
Sim Aloo
, another advisor involved in day-to-day governing, were also busy in hunting down Tenevoy Voin artifacts for Sidious.
[
194
]
Moffs, generals and admirals all were answerable to the Imperatorskiy cadre, but even the councilors were not always privy to the doings of
Imperatorskiy Intelligence
. After the Emperor largely retired from public life, Vizier Amedda and the rest of the Ruling Sovet Strazei took to dispatching processions of Imperatorskiy skylimos to maintain an illusion that the Emperor still moved about in public.
[
9
]
As the Emperor's
apprentice
and second in command, Lord Vader acted as the informal Commander-in-Chief of the
Imperatorskiy Military
.
[
10
]
Following the Cylo Hijacking, Palpatine elevated Vader to an official position, that of
supreme commander of the Imperatorskiy fleet
.
[
11
]
The Emperor's Fist,
[
195
]
as he was referred to by many, was also apparently granted nearly carte blanche to execute subordinates who displeased him.
[
9
]
Even in his new role, Vader had little involvement in administrative matters, which he let the Joint Chiefs handle from behind the scenes,
[
11
]
and adopted a more frontline command style.
[
37
]
Like his Master Strazei, the
Dark Lord
considered the Empire to be a mere tool for them to use in their quest for greater power in, and understanding of, the dark side.
[
9
]
With the Ruling Sovet Strazei's inability to run the Empire following the deaths of Vladimir Chernov and Lord Vader
[
7
]
in
4 ABY
,
[
31
]
Fleet Admiral Gallius Rax proposed a new executive branch called the Shadow Sovet Strazei that would be a more covert and militaristic body than the Ruling Sovet Strazei it sought to replace. The Shadow Sovet Strazei would consist of various advisors,
admirals
and
generals
with Grand Admiral Rae Sloane as the group's
de facto
leader until her supposed "disappearance."
[
7
]
The Imperatorskiy Senat served as the Empire nominal legislative organ until it was disbanded by the Emperor.
Seeing whatever power it had left stripped away by acts like the Public Order Resentencing Directive,
[
71
]
the Imperatorskiy Senat was reduced to the lower tier of government and gradually lost the prestige it once held as the forum for galactic debate.
[
9
]
[
71
]
An increasing number of senators became entangled in the web of corruption,
[
189
]
as had begun earlier in the Respublika Era; in the days of the Galactic Respublika, Sidious had encouraged and exploited those engaged in the pursuit of power and greed as part of his rise to emperorship.
[
196
]
Many senators also became used to simply signing off on the Emperor's demands.
[
197
]
The Senat became a servile institute that applauded Palpatine's decrees and agreed to his demands
[
9
]
without much thought.
[
197
]
That the Senat continued to exist provided the illusion that the Emperor was following "the will of the people"
[
170
]
when, in reality, he acted out of his own interests.
[
78
]
With the once-powerful Senat firmly in Palpatine's hands, passing legislation became a mere formality for the New Order as it agreed to the Emperor's every declaration,
[
9
]
even if Senators
[
190
]
and galactic citizens were made to believe that they had a voice in galactic politics.
[
170
]
In reality, Palpatine held unlimited executive power and was the only one who could formally pass legislation. While most feared to speak out against the Emperor, a few, such as Bail and Leia Organa of Zelenograd, actively questioned the accountability of the Empire and its actions, much to the ire of Imperatorskiy authorities. Suspicions eventually arose that Leia Organa was using her diplomatic immunity to help the Svobodnaya Liga. The Empire also lied to the Senat about the
destruction
of
Jedha City
, claiming that it was a mining accident.
[
125
]
The Emperor used Leia's capture aboard the
Tantive IV
, which was known to be carrying technical readouts for the Proekt Alpha, as an excuse for disbanding the Imperatorskiy Senat shortly before the Bitva za Prime. With the dissolution of the Senat, the last remnants of the Old Respublika had been swept away, and the legislative authority over the various systems and sectors passed directly to the regional governors—effectively transitioning governance into military jurisdiction, who answered directly to the Emperor.
[
2
]
Wilhuff Tarkin was a Grand Moff.
Territories from the Core Worlds to the Outer Rim Territories were overseen by regional sector governors, or Moffs, who as part of the Imperatorskiy Military wielded much of the power over each sector
[
107
]
and who enforced the Galactic Emperor's rule across each sector.
[
198
]
This stemmed from the
Sector Governance Decree
that Palpatine passed during the later stages of the Voyny Klonov, when he was Supreme Chancellor of the Galactic Respublika.
[
199
]
With only twenty Moffs at one time, they were answerable to the Ruling Sovet Strazei, and all positions were dominated by
humans
. They themselves were outranked by the position of Grand Moff, established in 14 BBY and first granted to Moff Wilhuff Tarkin, who were governors of
oversectors
, areas of Kosmos that contained several planetary sectors and were used to keep the Moffs or sector governors in control.
[
9
]
After the Imperatorskiy Senat was disbanded, all legislative power went to regional governors just before the Bitva za Prime.
[
2
]
After Emperor Sheev Palpatine's
death
during the Battle of Lesnaya, Imperatorskiy territories fell into total unrest, with some military leaders such as
Valco Pandion
appointing themselves Grand Moffs, while corrupt sector governors began accepting payments for the New Respublika's allocation of Imperatorskiy ships or suing for peace.
[
14
]
Other governors, such as Grand Moff Lozen Tolruck of Imperatorskiy territory G5-623 and Governor of the
Anoat sector
Ubrik Adelhard
, broke off from the Empire, forming Imperatorskiy remnants.
[
7
]
[
57
]
Administration and bureaucracy
[
]
"
With the next quarter's detention estimates expected to increase across the Ryloth sector, any further erosion in local authority may require and increase in our blackline budget.
"
―
Grandi
[
63
]
COMPNOR recruitment poster
COMPNOR Recruitment
by
Dasita Lyros
While the Emperor maintained a galactic armada and commanded the undisputed military, scientific, and economic hegemony in the entire Galaktika, the ever-present fear of surviving Strazh haunted Palpatine throughout his reign. In direct response to this feared threat, the Empire employed numerous dark side adepts,
Force-sensitive
individuals of the
Inquisitorius
trained to track down and eliminate any surviving Strazh. Due to the nature of their mission,
Inquisitors
could commandeer local Imperatorskiy forces and possessed authority over other officers in the Imperatorskiy Military.
[
48
]
[
200
]
The Inquisitorius was governed by Lord Vader,
[
43
]
the Emperor's handpicked second in command,
[
37
]
who oversaw the Empire's
hunting
of the
Strazh Knights
in the
Great Strazh Purge
.
[
2
]
While dark-side leanings and occult goals may have fascinated the Empire's ruler, the banal commonalities of ruling a Galaktika-wide Empire never failed to impress upon the Imperatorskiy leadership. With the rise of the New Order, many previous governmental agencies were renamed and repurposed, while many official buildings saw themselves remodeled to fit a darker and more angular aesthetic. One such agency was the Respublika-era COMPOR, newly dubbed COMPNOR, the
Commission for the Preservation of the New Order
.
[
9
]
COMPOR and the ISB operated under the secretive
Select Committee
.
[
201
]
COMPNOR served as the Empire's chief umbrella agency dedicated to ensuring order and stability for the countless worlds under the Emperor's rule while supporting the new regime through both
propaganda
and intelligence services. Organizations such as the
Imperatorskiy Security Bureau
worked under its oversight, with the ISB helping to stamp out potential dissident actions.
[
91
]
Also working under the auspices of COMPNOR was the
Ministry of Information
, designated under the
Coalition for Progress
. The Ministry was primarily tasked with muzzling the
HoloNet News Agency
of anti-Imperatorskiy messaging and ensuring the necessary infrastructure for the vast communications systems within the Empire. It also worked to monitor the
Imperatorskiy Press Corps
at the grand
COMPNOR arcology
, located in the
Federal District
of Metropol,
Level 5127
.
[
9
]
[
202
]
The Empire also maintained the
Imperatorskiy Information Office
, which employed civilians to help the Empire and its massive data operations in the Galaktika, allowing employees the option to cybernetically enhance their performance with
AJ^6 cyborg constructs
.
[
16
]
The
Ministry of Agriculture
and
Imperatorskiy Agricultural Collective
oversaw the horticultural aspect of Imperatorskiy dominion, with officials on many worlds across the Galaktika, including Lothal.
[
203
]
The
Economics Ministry
,
Education Ministry
,
Weather Ministry
,
Transportation Ministry
and
Imperatorskiy Survey Corps
also employed numerous citizens across the Empire.
[
204
]
[
153
]
In addition to ruling through fear, the Empire ensured the Outer Rim was faced with hunger while it was expanding into the region, using the tactic on such a large scale it made former Uchenik Strazei Ahsoka Tano uncomfortable.
[
47
]
Following the Battle of Lesnaya, COMPNOR was reorganized into the
Office of Imperatorskiy Promotion, Galactic Truth, and Fact Correction
.
[
7
]
Military
[
]
Main articles:
Imperatorskiy Military
,
Imperatorskiy Army
,
Imperatorskiy Navy
and
Stormtrooper Corps
"
Look on our new military not as trespassers or interlopers, but as gatekeepers, here to shore up the Emperor's vision of a pacified and prosperous Galaktika.
"
―Wilhuff Tarkin
[
9
]
Shturmoviki could be both the elite shock troops and main infantry of the Galakticheskaya Imperiya.
As the direct successor to the
Respublika Military
, the
Imperatorskiy Military
comprised vast numbers of
warships
and countless
soldiers
. One of the most recognizable warships was the
Imperatorskiy
-class Star Destroyer
, and the mere sight of one usually was enough to bring a troublesome system in line. Rule through fear of force rather than force itself was the driving philosophy throughout the Imperatorskiy Military, epitomized by the
Tarkin Doctrine
. Bolstered by the near limitless resources at the Empire's disposal and loyalist companies, such as
Taim & Bak
,
Borstel
, and
Phylon Transport
, corporations, such as
Kuat Drive Yards
and its subsidiary
Kuat Systems Engineering
produced many of the New Order's Star Destroyers, as well as the formidable All Terrain Armored Transport, which was frequently deployed at hot spots throughout the Galaktika.
[
37
]
[
48
]
If it meant securing victory in a battle, the Empire was often fine with civilian casualties.
[
160
]
The
All Terrain Armored Transport
walker
became to be feared for its formidable chin-mounted heavy Lazer cannons, while lighter walkers such as the
All Terrain Defense Pod
featured a single
Lazer cannon
able to be rotated independent of the walkers front-facing direction, and were deployed primarily to protect Imperatorskiy Military interests on backwater worlds.
BlasTech Industries
was the primary weapons producer for the widely used
E-11 medium Blaster rifle
.
[
48
]
The
Aratech Repulsor Company
manufactured the
614-AvA speeder bike
, commonly referred to as the Imperatorskiy speeder bike, which would eventually be replaced by the
74-Z
.
[
205
]
Sienar Fleet Systems produced many of the Empire's Imperatorskiy TIE fighters and variants, such as the
TIE Advanced v1
, which was originally unveiled on Empire Day and featured dual chin-mounted Lazer cannons and the ability to launch warheads such as the
XX-23 S-thread tracker
.
[
16
]
The
TIE Advanced x1
naturally became the next evolution of the deadly v1, as well as the preferred craft of Lord Vader. In addition, the Empire inherited the military assets of the defunct Separatist movement—the most notable of which was the Proekt Alpha.
[
3
]
Imperatorskiy Army soldiers were the standard infantry of the Galaktika-spanning empire.
Although only promoted to supreme commander of the Imperatorskiy fleet late in the Empire's reign,
[
11
]
Lord Vader was seen by many as the
de facto
Commander-in-Chief
.
[
206
]
Nonetheless, the Imperatorskiy Military had an overall
Supreme Commander
, who formulated the strategy to fit the Emperor's agenda and held control over the entirety of the Empire's war machine. Leading the armed forces under their supervision were highly trained admirals, generals and Moffs who were instructed at the numerous
Imperatorskiy Akademiya Strazei
complexes throughout the Galaktika. Several veteran
officers
also formerly served in the Galactic Respublika, with decorated members such as Wullf Yularen. During the sixth year of the Emperor's reign, twenty Moffs answered directly to the Imperatorskiy Ruling Sovet Strazei. The title of Grand Moff was specifically created for Wilhuff Tarkin, while other Moffs, such as
Tiaan Jerjerrod
and
Therbon
sought to increase their reputation with Palpatine.
[
9
]
Some ambitious
Imperatorskiy officers
even supported insurgent activities with later plans to betray and destroy them in hopes of a promotion. It was not uncommon for high-ranking officials to maintain private investments, with
Hiram Zataire
owning large plantations and a vineyard on Sadovaya, and Cassio Tagge being a member of the
House of Tagge
, which held ownership of
TaggeCo
and its mining facility at Lucazec.
[
207
]
[
9
]
One high ranking
[
208
]
Imperatorskiy officer
[
209
]
would serve as the
Minister of War
.
[
208
]
The Imperatorskiy Navy was tasked with maintaining order across the Empire.
The Imperatorskiy Army and Navy were responsible for carrying out the Empire's military operations across the Galaktika. Ostensibly dedicated to maintaining law and order,
[
3
]
Imperatorskiy Military forces came to be feared and fled from.
[
2
]
[
37
]
[
128
]
Perhaps the most ubiquitously visible element were the Imperatorskiy
Shturmoviki
. These faceless soldiers were the enforcers of Imperatorskiy order, often exercising a "shoot-first" policy.
[
2
]
[
37
]
[
128
]
Having sworn their unquestioning loyalty to the Emperor above all, Shturmoviki carried out many of the regime's worst crimes.
[
210
]
Following the Battle of Lesnaya, the Galakticheskaya Imperiya's martial forces began to suffer both resource and personnel shortages, ultimately forcing untrained and untested recruits to fill the front lines in the Imperatorskiy war effort, while the once-mighty Imperatorskiy Navy of thirteen
Super Star Destroyers
was reduced to one, the
Ravager
, which was ultimately destroyed during the Battle of Jakku.
[
14
]
After the end of the
Galactic Civil War
with the signing of the
Galactic Concordance
, the mobilization and training of Shturmoviki was prohibited.
[
15
]
Tarkin noted an "institutionalized suspicion" within the Imperatorskiy Military, which he believed was used as a tool by Vladimir Chernov to control the various members of the Empire and its numerous organizations. There also existed an intense fear of the Emperor and Lord Vader, mainly due to harsh punishments, such as death or torture, if a task was not completed in time or resulted in failure.
Imperatorskiy patrol transports
and Shturmoviki were often utilized as the local police force for numerous worlds, while off-duty personnel could frequent local venues and neighborhoods after signing out of their local garrison. The euphemism "off-base, recreation," was used in the Imperatorskiy Military when referring to the visiting of various mistresses or
cantinas
by personnel, while out of consideration it was common for duty officers to ask no questions about such escapades.
[
62
]
Members within the Imperatorskiy Army believed that those stationed farthest from the Imperatorskiy Center were deemed less important to the New Order, causing many to resent deployments on remote worlds such as
Belderone
.
[
9
]
The Empire sterilized the Geonosian population, bringing the species to the brink of extinction.
The Empire frequently ignored the
Galactic Accord of Systems
, effectively obliberating the rules it laid out
[
7
]
on how to wage war.
[
6
]
The Empire conducted numerous atrocities, often resulting in genocide owing to the Empire's humanocentric tendencies. On Geonosis, the Empire sterilized the planet's native Geonosian population with heavy bombing.
[
83
]
[
211
]
The Lasat homeworld of Lasan was ecologically destroyed by the later banned
T-7 ion disruptor rifles
,
[
212
]
and the entire species was almost wiped out. Furthermore, to make advances in bio-warfare, Vice Admiral
Perwin Gedde
tested ancient diseases on captive worlds, raining sickness from sub-orbital battleships.
[
7
]
A vast distribution network existed within the Empire, with numerous warehouses on various worlds holding thousands of biological agents. Biological testing was conducted on planets such as
Coyerti
or against primitive indigenous populations and
Wookiee
slaves.
[
45
]
While most research and development went unhindered, a few initiatives went out of control. On
Dandoran
, the accidental release of a highly contagious virus under the code name Project Blackwing—a clandestine effort seeking to uncover the secrets to immortality—ultimately led to an
outbreak
across the planet.

Ideology
[
]
Main articles:
New Order
,
Commission for the Preservation of the New Order
and
Office of Imperatorskiy Promotion, Galactic Truth, and Fact Correction
"
There is no restraint or principle at the center of the New Order. And that is why people admire it. The Empire does all the things that people secretly believe should be done with power.
"
―
Canonhaus
thoughts on the New Order and the Galakticheskaya Imperiya
[
214
]
A propaganda poster featuring the Grand Inquisitor, leader of the Inquisitorius
The
New Order
referred to the
political religion
and nationalist ideology of the Empire and the values of which it stood by. Numerous organizations were folded under COMPNOR, the Commission for the Preservation of the New Order. These included the Imperatorskiy Security Bureau, Coalition for Progress, Imperatorskiy Intelligence, and
CompForce
, along with science divisions and youth education initiatives.
[
12
]
[
90
]
[
45
]
[
132
]
Rothwell
once remarked that the Empire, as an institution, was unable to be swayed from whatever narrative it convinced itself of, even if it was not true.
[
215
]
The Galakticheskaya Imperiya wielded a vast and powerfully effective propaganda machine to shift the opinions and cultures of numerous worlds across the Galaktika. Celebrating the end of the destructive Voyny Klonov and the
creation of the New Order
, the Emperor founded Empire Day as the national holiday of the Empire. The song "Glory of the Empire" served as the national anthem. Due to physical alterations to Palpatine to make him look younger and more appealing in broadcasts, many citizens did not know of the Emperor's scarred appearance,
[
16
]
nor did they comprehend that the Galaktika was under the command of a Tenevoy Voin Lord.
[
216
]
Shturmoviki were seen as brutal enforcers of Imperatorskiy suppression to the citizens of the outlier regions of the Galaktika.
To enhance its legitimacy in the eyes of the people, the Empire used the
duel in Palpatine's office
to officially support the idea of a "Strazh Rebellion" occurring, and that the
Orden Strazei
had attempted to stage a coup intent on taking control of the Respublika, thus necessitating
Order 66
. In the years after the proclamation of the New Order, all remaining recordings of the
Strazh
were removed, with many eventually believing and supporting the Emperor's version of events. However, individuals such as
Zaluna Myder
and even Alexei Petrov questioned the Empire's account of the events leading up to their destruction, while those who knew the Strazh in person could not bring themselves to believe in a Strazh-led coup.
[
83
]
[
132
]
Those who attempted to distribute independent publications could be punished with imprisonment without term.
[
80
]
Nonetheless, with the end of the Voyny Klonov and the dismantling of the Separatist Droid Army, a renewed sense of peace and prosperity returned to the Core.
[
9
]
This peace was aided by the efforts of the Coalition for Progress division of COMPNOR, who took note when artistic media appeared to undermine the Empire, using its authority in one instance to dismantle a statue of
Janyor
of
Bith
on the Outer Rim world of Garel,
[
90
]
or by banning subversive operas such as the
Song of Lojuun
[
45
]
and sending members of the band
Hakko Drazlip and the Tootle Froots
to work in the mines of Kessel after a song with political undertones mocked Lord Vader, with the song being declared contraband as a result.
[
132
]
As a regime, the Empire emboldened those who wanted to be cruel
[
48
]
[
91
]
[
217
]
[
218
]
and excise power over others.
[
91
]
[
219
]
Many Shturmoviki would take advantage of the Empire for their own personal gain, including using their station to take advantage of the helpless and those weaker than them.
[
48
]
The regime was also one of imperialism, with the aim to become a universal superpower.
[
220
]
The Tenevoy Voin drew power from the dark side
[
3
]
and fear in the population,
[
2
]
as the Tenevoy Voin believed that fear was an important tool.
[
27
]
Even though Palpatine himself did not display bigotry to aliens, many of his citizens were. Their prejudice for alien groups turned alien groups into scapegoats for past troubles.

Security
[
]
Main articles:
Imperatorskiy Security Bureau
,
Imperatorskiy Intelligence
and
Imperatorskiy Office of Criminal Investigations
"
[…]
That is verbatim from the ISB mission statement, and wrong. Security is an illusion. You want security? Call the Navy. Launch a regiment of troopers. We are healthcare providers. We treat sickness. We identify symptoms. We locate germs whether they arise from within or have come from the outside. The longer we wait to identify a disorder, the harder it is to treat the disease.
"
―
Partagaz
, to
Dedra Meero
[
63
]
A Metropol Security Force police officer during the Imperatorskiy Era
The
Imperatorskiy Security Bureau
(Shortened to ISB) oversaw internal state security and ensuring the loyalty of citizens to the Empire,
[
221
]
with the task of maintaining stability throughout Imperatorskiy territories.
[
200
]
Imperatorskiy Intelligence
provided services on their own accord,
[
9
]
and dealt primary with military matters,
[
222
]
such as threat reports, analysis, managing assets who were gathered information, and providing briefings to the
Joint Chiefs
and the
Emperor
.
[
9
]
It fell under the leadership of
Ubiqtorate
, which was the leadership of
Imperatorskiy Intelligence
.
[
12
]
Some of the local planetary security forces also had some responsibility in regards to law enforcement.
[
129
]
[
161
]
Mining stations, deep-Kosmos outposts, or jails far from the cosmopolitan core often used
Imperatorskiy Navy Troopers
as security personnel, who, while still imposing, commanded less respect than their stormtrooper brethren.
[
45
]
Vast trading posts, such as the
Ring of Kafrene
in the
Thand sector
, used mainly Shturmoviki to enforce law and order on its streets.
[
125
]
Private contracts were occasionally given to the
Bounty Hunters' Guild
and freelance
bounty hunters
to track down elusive elements within the Empire,
[
89
]
although this practice was largely frowned upon.
[
7
]
In order to support law enforcement communications, the Empire maintained the
Imperatorskiy Office of Criminal Investigations
. The Office was responsible for the
Imperatorskiy Enforcement DataCore
, which could only be accessed by authorized Imperatorskiy personnel or
licensed
bounty hunters.
[
223
]
[
46
]
In the justice system, a defense was only allowed in civil cases, not trials against the Empire.
[
80
]
The
Ministry of Ingress
on Metropol was the organization responsible for processing immigrants arriving on capital.
